/def/vehicle/truck/scania.s_2016/interior/sound_effects_units.sui:1: # Configuration of the sound effects for the interior reverb and the windows opening sound equalizations.
/def/vehicle/truck/scania.s_2016/interior/sound_effects_units.sui:4: # .effect.equalizer.windows.closed / .opened = 3-eq settings for fully closed / opened windows for central (interior) sound.
